Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in number_theory theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 17 and the invariant I(A,B) = 192 mod 4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to number_theory structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 17/192 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the number_theory space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 718 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 187. QED.
